     1	import { safeReadJson } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     2	
     3	// Doctor's probe results live in .handoff/log/probe-cache.json for 15 minutes
     4	// (plan §Availability probing). This reader is the consumption side (gate-2
     5	// major 13): remap's resolver and receive's token derivation take the FRESH
     6	// cache; a stale or absent cache reads as null — offline resolution stays
     7	// possible, flagged degraded by the resolver.
     8	
     9	export const PROBE_CACHE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    13	 * @returns {Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}
    14	 */
    15	export function readProbeCache(root, io) {
    16	  const cached = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/.handoff/log/probe-cache.json`);
    17	  if (!cached.ok || typeof cached.value?.at !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(cached.value.records)) return null;
    18	  if (!(Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(cached.value.at) < PROBE_CACHE_MS)) return null;
    19	  /** @type {Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}>} */
    20	  const out = {};
    21	  for (const r of cached.value.records) {
    22	    // capability null in a probe record means the binary was not found — a
    23	    // VERIFIED not-installed, which the resolver must skip, not select.
    24	    if (r && typeof r.platform === 'string') out[r.platform] = { capability: r.capability ?? 'not-installed', outcome: r.outcome ?? 'error' };
    25	  }
    26	  return out;
    27	}

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Loop failover orchestration — what the supervisor does when a child dies.
     3	 * Classifies the death via the signature table, then takes exactly one path:
     4	 * retry-once-then-park (generic failures), entry-level re-resolve
     5	 * (model-unavailable — the platform survives its dead model), or the
     6	 * usage-limit transaction: checkpoint the loop position INTO the bundle,
     7	 * seal, and receive onto the resolver's pick with one bounded stale retry.
     8	 * Writes NOTHING under .handoff/loop/ — the supervisor applies the returned
     9	 * decision to loop state afterward. Plan:
    10	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Limit failover" +
    11	 * §"Model-level failover".
    12	 */
    13	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
    14	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
    15	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
    16	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
    17	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
    18	import { loadBundle } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
    19	import { snapshot as gitSnapshot } from '../git/snapshot.mjs';
    20	import { prepare, commit } from '../receive/txn.mjs';
    21	import { cmdCheckpoint } from '../commands/checkpoint.mjs';
    22	import { cmdFinalize } from '../commands/finalize.mjs';
    23	
    24	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    25	
    26	/**
    27	 * @param {{
    28	 *   root: string, io: any, config: any, role: string,
    29	 *   assignment: {platform: string, role?: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string},
    30	 *   transcript: string, exitCode: number, sessionHint: string,
    31	 *   probes?: any, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>,
    32	 *   attempt: number, loopState?: any, degradedOpen?: boolean,
    33	 * }} input
    34	 * @returns {Promise<
    35	 *   | {action: 'retry', class: string, attempt: number}
    36	 *   | {action: 'park', class: string, reason: string, resumeAt?: string}
    37	 *   | {action: 'relaunch', class: string, assignment: any, avoidEntries?: any[], prompt?: string}
    38	 * >}
    39	 */
    40	export async function runFailover(input) {
    41	  const { root, io, config, role, assignment, sessionHint } = input;
    42	  const avoid = input.avoid ?? [];
    43	  const avoidEntries = input.avoidEntries ?? [];
    44	
    45	  // Classification is TABLE-driven — the same loader/classifier as `baton
    46	  // detect`, so overlay/fixture signatures are honored, never hardcoded.
    47	  const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    48	  const verdict = classify({ text: input.transcript, exitCode: input.exitCode, platform: assignment.platform, table });
    49	
    50	  // Model-level failure: the platform is fine, one model is dead. Avoid the
    51	  // exact {platform, model} tuple and re-resolve — no seal, no receive.
    52	  if (verdict.class === 'model-unavailable') {
    53	    const nextAvoidEntries = [...avoidEntries, { platform: assignment.platform, model: assignment.model }];
    54	    const resolved = resolveRoles({ config, to: assignment.platform, avoid, avoidEntries: nextAvoidEntries, probes: input.probes ?? null });
    55	    const next = resolved.assignments[role];
    56	    if (!next || next.mode === 'unavailable') {
    57	      return { action: 'park', class: verdict.class, reason: `no eligible chain entry left for role '${role}' after avoiding ${assignment.platform}/${assignment.model}` };
    58	    }
    59	    return { action: 'relaunch', class: verdict.class, assignment: next, avoidEntries: nextAvoidEntries };
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  // Anything that is not a usage-limit death is a child failure, not a
    63	  // platform death: retry once, then park. Never a seal or receive.
    64	  if (verdict.class !== 'usage-limit') {
    65	    if (input.attempt <= 1) return { action: 'retry', class: verdict.class, attempt: input.attempt };
    66	    return { action: 'park', class: verdict.class, reason: `child failed twice (${verdict.class}) — parked for operator review` };
    67	  }
    68	
    69	  // ---- Usage-limit: the exact transaction (plan steps 3–8). ----------------
    70	  const { bundle } = loadBundle(root, io);
    71	  const originPlatform = bundle?.origin?.platform ?? assignment.platform;
    72	
    73	  // (3) Checkpoint the loop position INTO the bundle under the supervisor
    74	  // session BEFORE any seal, so the frozen seal carries it.
    75	  if (input.loopState !== undefined) {
    76	    const checkpointStdin = JSON.stringify({
    77	      schema: 'baton/event@1',
    78	      events: [{ type: 'decision', payload: { summary: `loop position at limit death: ${JSON.stringify(input.loopState)}` } }],
    79	    });
    80	    const checkpointArgs = ['--platform', originPlatform, '--session', sessionHint, '--root', root];
    81	    // Hook-safety makes a foreign-session rejection EXIT 0, so it is detected
    82	    // from the message, not the code. When the active bundle belongs to
    83	    // another session (e.g. the operator's own hooks — dogfood finding D3),
    84	    // the supervised run must take it over before sealing: it cannot seal
    85	    // state it does not own. A same-session bundle never triggers the retry.
    86	    let firstStderr = '';
    87	    await cmdCheckpoint(checkpointArgs, {
    88	      ...io,
    89	      stdin: checkpointStdin,
    90	      stderr: { write: (/** @type {string} */ s) => { firstStderr += s; return true; } },
    91	    });
    92	    if (firstStderr) io.stderr.write(firstStderr);
    93	    if (/foreign session/i.test(firstStderr)) {
    94	      await cmdCheckpoint([...checkpointArgs, '--take-over'], { ...io, stdin: checkpointStdin });
    95	    }
    96	  }
    97	
    98	  // (4) Seal — unless the caller knows a clean seal is impossible
    99	  // (degradedOpen: a prior death left the bundle unsealed; receive's
   100	  // degraded-open path compensates), or the bundle is already sealed.
   101	  const alreadySealed = bundle?.handoff?.status === 'sealed';
   102	  if (input.degradedOpen !== true && !alreadySealed) {
   103	    await cmdFinalize(['--reason', 'usage-limit death during a supervised loop run', '--reason-class', 'usage-limit', '--root', root], io);
   104	  }
   105	
   106	  // (7, ordered before receive because receive needs the target) — resolver
   107	  // pick with the dead origin platform avoided.
   108	  const relaunchAvoid = avoid.includes(assignment.platform) ? avoid : [...avoid, assignment.platform];
   109	  const resolved = resolveRoles({ config, to: assignment.platform, avoid: relaunchAvoid, avoidEntries, probes: input.probes ?? null });
   110	  const target = resolved.assignments[role];
   111	  if (!target || target.mode === 'unavailable') {
   112	    return {
   113	      action: 'park',
   114	      class: verdict.class,
   115	      reason: `every platform is avoided or unavailable for role '${role}' — parked with a resume hint`,
   116	      ...(verdict.resetHint ? { resumeAt: verdict.resetHint } : {}),
   117	    };
   118	  }
   119	
   120	  // (5–6) Receive prepare+commit back-to-back with IDENTICAL intake and the
   121	  // supervisor session hint. Git is re-derived fresh for each phase (exactly
   122	  // as the CLI does), so real drift inside the window stales the token; ONE
   123	  // automatic re-prepare retry, then park. No .handoff/loop writes anywhere.
   124	  const receiveOpts = (/** @type {any} */ git) => ({
   125	    platform: target.platform,
   126	    origin: 'unknown', // defaults from the sealed bundle's recorded origin
   127	    reason: 'unspecified', // defaults from the sealed reason
   128	    reasonClass: 'usage-limit',
   129	    gitSnapshot: git,
   130	    probes: input.probes ?? null,
   131	    sessionHint,
   132	    sessionUnstable: false,
   133	  });
   134	
   135	  /** @type {string} */
   136	  let prompt = '';
   137	  let lastError = null;
   138	  for (let window = 1; window <= 2; window += 1) {
   139	    const prepGit = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   140	    const prepared = prepare(root, receiveOpts(prepGit), io);
   141	    prompt = prepared.prompt;
   142	    const commitGit = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   143	    try {
   144	      commit(root, prepared.token, receiveOpts(commitGit), io);
   145	      lastError = null;

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/roles/resolve.mjs | sed -n '1,125p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Pure, explainable role resolution over the committed matrix. Every decision
     3	 * is auditable: chosen entries carry their chainIndex; passed-over entries land
     4	 * in skipped[] with a why. avoid[] population is the caller's job.
     5	 * @param {{config: any, to: string, avoid?: string[], avoidEntries?: Array<{platform: string, model: string}>, nativeOnly?: boolean, probes?: Record<string, {capability: string, outcome: string}> | null}} input
     6	 * @returns {{assignments: Record<string, any>, notes: string[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function resolveRoles({ config, to, avoid = [], avoidEntries = [], nativeOnly = false, probes = null }) {
     9	  /** @type {string[]} */
    10	  const notes = [];
    11	
    12	  /**
    13	   * The eligibility verdict for a platform, or null when eligible.
    14	   * @param {string} platform @returns {string | null}
    15	   */
    16	  function skipReason(platform) {
    17	    if (avoid.includes(platform)) return 'avoided';
    18	    if (!(platform in (config.platforms ?? {}))) return 'unknown-platform';
    19	    if (config.platforms[platform]?.enabled === false) return 'disabled';
    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
    31	    return null;
    32	  }
    33	
    34	  // A selection is degraded when its availability is UNVERIFIED (gate-2 iter-2
    35	  // M3, iter-3 F1): with no probe cache at all, resolution is offline and EVERY
    36	  // selection is degraded (plan: "Offline resolution always succeeds, flagged
    37	  // degraded"); with a partial cache, platforms the cache is missing are; and a
    38	  // platform probed only to 'installed' (auth unverified, e.g. a --version
    39	  // success) is degraded even though it stays selectable.
    40	  /** @param {string} platform */
    41	  const probeUnverified = (platform) => {
    42	    if (probes == null || !probes[platform]) return true;
    43	    const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    44	    // Degraded when ANY required dimension is unverified: a capped 'installed'
    45	    // (auth never confirmed) OR a non-ok outcome (iter-4 I6) — a selectable
    46	    // platform whose last probe ERRORED or TIMED OUT is presented as
    47	    // reachability-verified otherwise, which is exactly the silent-degradation
    48	    // the plan's degraded flag exists to surface. (rate-limited is skipped, not
    49	    // selected, so it never reaches here.)
    50	    return capability === 'installed' || outcome !== 'ok';
    51	  };
    52	
    53	  // Entry-level avoidance (plan §Model-level failover): a specific
    54	  // {platform, model} tuple is dead (e.g. account-tier rejected) while its
    55	  // platform stays usable. Matching is on model IDENTITY — an @effort suffix
    56	  // on either side is ignored — and requires BOTH dimensions, so the same
    57	  // model name on another platform is never caught.
    58	  /** @param {any} m */
    59	  const baseModel = (m) => (typeof m === 'string' ? m.split('@')[0] : m);
    60	  /** @param {string} platform @param {string} model */
    61	  const entryAvoided = (platform, model) =>
    62	    avoidEntries.some((a) => a?.platform === platform && baseModel(a?.model) === baseModel(model));
    63	
    64	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    65	  const assignments = {};
    66	  for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(config.roles)) {
    67	    /** @type {any[]} */
    68	    const skipped = [];
    69	    /** @type {any} */
    70	    let assignment = null;
    71	
    72	    for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    73	      const entry = chain[i];
    74	      if (nativeOnly && entry.platform !== to) {
    75	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'non-native' });
    76	        continue;
    77	      }
    78	      const why = skipReason(entry.platform);
    79	      if (why) {
    80	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why });
    81	        continue;
    82	      }
    83	      // After skipReason so a dual match keeps the platform-level label.
    84	      if (entryAvoided(entry.platform, entry.model)) {
    85	        skipped.push({ chainIndex: i, platform: entry.platform, model: entry.model, why: 'avoided-entry' });
    86	        continue;
    87	      }
    88	      assignment = {
    89	        platform: entry.platform,
    90	        model: entry.model,
    91	        effort: entry.effort ?? null,
    92	        mode: entry.platform === to ? 'native' : 'delegated',
    93	        chainIndex: i,
    94	        skipped,
    95	      };
    96	      if (probeUnverified(entry.platform)) assignment.degraded = true;
    97	      break;
    98	    }
    99	
   100	    if (!assignment && nativeOnly) {
   101	      // Forced-default is only valid when the destination itself is eligible.
   102	      const whyTo = skipReason(to);
   103	      const model = config.defaults?.[to];
   104	      if (!whyTo && typeof model === 'string') {
   105	        assignment = { platform: to, model, effort: null, mode: 'forced-default', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   106	        if (probeUnverified(to)) assignment.degraded = true;
   107	      }
   108	    }
   109	
   110	    if (!assignment) {
   111	      assignment = { platform: null, model: null, effort: null, mode: 'unavailable', chainIndex: null, skipped };
   112	    }
   113	    assignments[role] = assignment;
   114	  }
   115	
   116	  return { assignments, notes };
   117	}

codex
Audit result: the cache shape/freshness pins are mostly sound, and the four reds are behavioral assertion failures. Typecheck is clean, and the two command suites fail at the intended item-8 assertions.

The blocker is coverage: plan item 8 requires cache propagation into every failover relaunch across loop and pipeline, including writer, reviewer, and merger paths, but the new tests only prove pipeline writer failover.   1035	// D6 (dogfood milestone C, attempt 2) — reviewer and merger children get the
  1036	// SAME classify→failover treatment as writers. Live: the subtask-reviewer
  1037	// inherits the other seat's worker chain whose head was a dead model; each
  1038	// unparseable death counted as a BLOCKED review and burned the whole gate cap.
  1039	// A failure-class death must re-resolve the role (model-unavailable → entry
  1040	// avoid), NOT consume a gate iteration, and park on chain exhaustion.
  1041	describe('pipeline — D6: reviewer & merger get classify→failover like writers', () => {
  1042	  const MU_LOG = `working…\n${MODEL_UNAVAIL}\n`;
  1043	  const approve = () => ({ verdict: 'APPROVED', exitCode: 0 });
  1044	  const deathMU = () => ({ verdict: 'BLOCKED', exitCode: 1, logContent: MU_LOG });
  1045	  // A child runner keyed on the spawn's --model. handlers[model] is a function
  1046	  // called per spawn (closures model queues); the default is APPROVED. Throws
  1047	  // past `bound` so an unbounded re-resolution loop terminates the test RED.
  1048	  function byModelRunner(io, handlers, { bound = 40 } = {}) {
  1049	    const calls = [];
  1050	    const fn = (spec, opts) => {
  1051	      const args = (spec?.args ?? []).map(String);
  1052	      const model = args[args.indexOf('--model') + 1];
  1053	      calls.push({ spec, command: spec?.command, args, model });
  1054	      if (calls.length > bound) throw new Error(`unbounded failover: exceeded ${bound} spawns`);
  1055	      const h = handlers[model] ?? approve;
  1056	      const r = h();
  1057	      if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
  1058	        io.fs.mkdirSync(opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/')), { recursive: true });
  1059	        io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
  1060	      }
  1061	      return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });
  1062	    };
  1063	    fn.calls = calls;
  1064	    return fn;
  1065	  }
  1066	  const reviewerModels = (runner) => reviewersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1067	  const mergersOf = (runner) => runner.calls.filter((c) => argsOf(c).join(' ').includes('You are the merger'));
  1068	  const mergerModels = (runner) => mergersOf(runner).map((c) => valAfter(argsOf(c), '--model'));
  1069	  const MERGES = `${loopPaths('/repo').dir}/merges.ndjson`;
  1070	  const mergeReceipts = (io) => {
  1071	    const raw = io.files()[MERGES];
  1072	    return typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1073	  };
  1074	  // Transitions are journaled — a re-resolved death must leave NO gate-iteration
  1075	  // event, not merely a snapshot counter that happens to read 0.
  1076	  const gateIterationEvents = (io, gate) => {
  1077	    const raw = io.files()[loopPaths('/repo').journal];
  1078	    const events = typeof raw === 'string' ? raw.split('\n').filter((l) => l.trim() !== '').map((l) => JSON.parse(l)) : [];
  1079	    return events.filter((e) => e.type === 'gate-iteration' && e.gate === gate);

   110	/**
   111	 * A STRICT, stateful fake git: unstubbed commands reject; worktree add/list and
   112	 * checkout -b are modeled so setup + preflight/postflight + merge cohere.
   113	 * `over` overrides specific responses (e.g. a merge conflict).
   114	 */
   115	function pipelineGit({ over = {}, log = CLEAN_LOG, existingBranches = [], dirtyCwds = [], mergedBranches = [] } = {}) {
   116	  const added = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // path -> branch
   117	  const branchAt = /** @type {Map<string,string>} */ (new Map()); // cwd -> current branch
   118	  const branches = new Set(existingBranches); // stale/pre-existing namespaced branches
   119	  const dirty = new Set(dirtyCwds); // seat cwds whose index is dirty
   120	  const deleted = new Set(); // seats listed by git but raw-deleted on disk
   121	  const merged = new Set(mergedBranches); // branches already merged into main (ancestor; empty main..branch)
   122	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   123	  const lockProbeRef = { fn: /** @type {null | (() => boolean)} */ (null) };
   124	  let mainSha = MAIN_SHA; // mutable so a child can "move main" mid-subtask
   125	
   126	  const list = () => {
   127	    const entries = [{ path: '/repo', branch: 'main' }];
   128	    for (const [path, branch] of added) entries.push({ path, branch: branchAt.get(path) ?? branch });
   129	    return entries.map((e) => `worktree ${e.path}\nHEAD ${MAIN_SHA}\nbranch refs/heads/${e.branch}\n`).join('\n');
   130	  };
   131	
   132	  const fn = (/** @type {string} */ cmd, /** @type {string[]} */ args = [], /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   133	    const argstr = args.join(' ');
   134	    calls.push({ cmd, args, argstr, cwd: opts?.cwd, lockPresent: lockProbeRef.fn ? lockProbeRef.fn() : undefined });
   135	    if (cmd !== 'git') return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('spawn ENOENT'), { code: 'ENOENT' }));
   136	
   137	    // Explicit overrides win.
   138	    const okey = Object.keys(over).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).find((k) => argstr.includes(k));
   139	    if (okey) {
   140	      const r = over[okey];
   141	      if (r?.reject) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(r.stderr || 'git failed'), { code: r.code ?? 1, stdout: r.stdout ?? '', stderr: r.stderr ?? '' }));
   142	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: r?.stdout ?? '', stderr: r?.stderr ?? '' });
   143	    }
   144	
   145	    // A raw-deleted seat: git still LISTS it, but any command IN that cwd fails
   146	    // (the directory is gone). Only self-heal (prune + re-add) recovers it.
   147	    if (opts?.cwd && deleted.has(opts.cwd) && !argstr.startsWith('worktree ')) {
   148	      return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: cannot chdir to '${opts.cwd}': No such file or directory`), { code: 128, stderr: 'No such file or directory\n' }));
   149	    }
   150	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree list')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: list(), stderr: '' });
   151	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree add')) {
   152	      const path = args.find((a) => a.startsWith('/repo/.worktrees/'));
   153	      const bi = args.indexOf('-b');
   154	      const branch = bi >= 0 ? args[bi + 1] : 'baton/wt-x/base';
   155	      if (path) { added.set(path, branch); branchAt.set(path, branch); deleted.delete(path); }
   156	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   157	    }
   158	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree prune')) { deleted.clear(); return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }); }
   159	    if (argstr.startsWith('worktree remove')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   160	    if (argstr.startsWith('checkout -b')) {
   161	      const target = args[args.indexOf('-b') + 1];
   162	      if (branches.has(target)) return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists`), { code: 128, stderr: `fatal: a branch named '${target}' already exists\n` }));
   163	      branches.add(target);
   164	      if (opts?.cwd) branchAt.set(opts.cwd, target);
   165	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   166	    }
   167	    if (argstr.startsWith('status --porcelain')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: dirty.has(opts?.cwd) ? ' M dirty.txt\n' : '', stderr: '' });
   168	    if (argstr === 'rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD') return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${branchAt.get(opts?.cwd) ?? 'main'}\n`, stderr: '' });
   169	    if (argstr.startsWith('rev-parse')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: `${mainSha}\n`, stderr: '' });
   170	    // merge-base --is-ancestor <branch> main : exit 0 iff <branch> is already in main.
   171	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge-base --is-ancestor')) {
   172	      const b = args[args.indexOf('--is-ancestor') + 1];
   173	      return merged.has(b) ? Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' }) : Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error('not an ancestor'), { code: 1, stdout: '', stderr: '' }));
   174	    }
   175	    if (argstr.startsWith('log ')) {
   176	      // A merged branch has NO commits ahead of main (empty main..<branch>).
   177	      const range = args.find((a) => /\.\./.test(String(a))) ?? '';
   178	      const branch = String(range).split('..')[1];
   179	      if (branch && merged.has(branch)) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   180	      return Promise.resolve({ stdout: log, stderr: '' });
   181	    }
   182	    if (argstr.startsWith('merge')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: 'Fast-forward', stderr: '' });
   183	    if (argstr.startsWith('branch -D')) return Promise.resolve({ stdout: '', stderr: '' });
   184	    return Promise.reject(Object.assign(new Error(`unstubbed git: ${argstr}`), { code: 'ENOSTUB' }));
   185	  };
   186	  fn.calls = calls;
   187	  fn.git = () => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git');
   188	  fn.issued = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.some((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   189	  fn.matching = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr));
   190	  fn.withCwd = (/** @type {RegExp} */ re, /** @type {string} */ cwd) => calls.filter((c) => c.cmd === 'git' && re.test(c.argstr) && c.cwd === cwd);

    80	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    81	 * @returns {Promise<string>}

    83	async function deriveTrunk(root, io) {
    84	  try {
    85	    const out = String((await io.execFile('git', ['symbolic-ref', 'refs/remotes/origin/HEAD'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
    86	    const m = out.match(/refs\/remotes\/origin\/(.+)$/);
    87	    if (m?.[1]) return m[1];
    88	  } catch {
    89	    // No origin HEAD (local-only repo) — fall back to the checked-out branch.
    90	  }
    91	  const branch = String((await io.execFile('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], { cwd: root })).stdout).trim();
    92	  return branch.length > 0 && branch !== 'HEAD' ? branch : 'main';
    93	}
    94	
    95	/** Validate the spec's subtasks — every rejection names its offender.
    96	 * @param {any} subtasks @returns {string | null} */
    97	function validateSubtasks(subtasks) {
    98	  if (!Array.isArray(subtasks) || subtasks.length === 0) {
    99	    return 'loop.json needs a non-empty `subtasks` array ([{id, title}]) for a pipeline run';
   100	  }
   101	  const seen = new Set();
   102	  for (let i = 0; i < subtasks.length; i += 1) {
   103	    const st = subtasks[i];
   104	    if (typeof st?.id !== 'string' || st.id.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] is missing a string id`;
   105	    if (typeof st?.title !== 'string' || st.title.length === 0) return `subtasks[${i}] ('${st.id}') is missing a string title`;
   106	    if (seen.has(st.id)) return `subtasks carry a duplicate id '${st.id}' — ids name branches and must be unique`;
   107	    seen.add(st.id);
   108	  }
   109	  return null;
   110	}

   206	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   207	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   208	    calls.push({ spec, opts, command: spec?.command, args: spec?.args ?? [], env: spec?.env });
   209	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { verdict: 'APPROVED' };
   210	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string' && opts.logPath) {
   211	      const dir = opts.logPath.slice(0, opts.logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   212	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   213	      io.fs.writeFileSync(opts.logPath, r.logContent);
   214	    }
   215	    // A child may perturb the world (e.g. move main) between spawn and postflight.
   216	    if (typeof r.effect === 'function') r.effect();
   217	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath: opts.logPath });

    94	/**
    95	 * Scripted fake child runner honoring the injection seam. Each call shifts the
    96	 * next result off the queue, writes its logContent to opts.logPath (so the
    97	 * command reads the transcript from the child's LOG file), and records the
    98	 * spawn spec.
    99	 */
   100	function fakeRunner(io, script) {
   101	  const queue = [...script];
   102	  const calls = /** @type {any[]} */ ([]);
   103	  const fn = (/** @type {any} */ spec, /** @type {any} */ opts = {}) => {
   104	    calls.push({ spec, opts, args: spec?.args ?? [], command: spec?.command, env: spec?.env });
   105	    const r = queue.shift() ?? { timedOut: false, exitCode: 0, verdict: 'APPROVED', findings: '' };
   106	    // No fallback: the command MUST pass a real logPath (asserted below).
   107	    const logPath = opts.logPath;
   108	    if (typeof r.logContent === 'string') {
   109	      const dir = logPath.slice(0, logPath.lastIndexOf('/'));
   110	      io.fs.mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
   111	      io.fs.writeFileSync(logPath, r.logContent);
   112	    }
   113	    return Promise.resolve({ timedOut: r.timedOut ?? false, exitCode: r.exitCode ?? 0, verdict: r.verdict ?? 'APPROVED', findings: r.findings ?? '', logPath });
   114	  };
   115	  fn.calls = calls;
   116	  return fn;
   117	}

     1	/**
     2	 * Load + validate baton.config.json, parsing role chains from their
     3	 * "platform/model[@effort]" string form into structured entries.
     4	 * Never throws: returns {config, errors} with dotted-path errors.
     5	 * @param {string} cwd @param {any} io
     6	 * @returns {{config: any | null, errors: {path: string, msg: string}[]}}
     7	 */
     8	export function loadConfig(cwd, io) {
     9	  const file = `${cwd}/baton.config.json`;
    10	  /** @type {{path: string, msg: string}[]} */
    11	  const errors = [];
    12	
    13	  let raw;
    14	  try {
    15	    raw = io.fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
    16	  } catch {
    17	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json not found at ${file} — run 'baton init' to scaffold it` }] };
    18	  }
    19	  let parsed;
    20	  try {
    21	    parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    22	  } catch (err) {
    23	    return { config: null, errors: [{ path: '', msg: `baton.config.json is not valid JSON (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'parse error'})` }] };
    24	  }
    25	
    26	  if (parsed.roles === null || typeof parsed.roles !== 'object' || Array.isArray(parsed.roles)) {
    27	    errors.push({ path: 'roles', msg: 'roles must be an object mapping role names to chain arrays' });
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  /** @type {Record<string, any[]>} */
    31	  const roles = {};
    32	  if (errors.length === 0) {
    33	    for (const [role, chain] of Object.entries(parsed.roles)) {
    34	      if (!Array.isArray(chain)) {
    35	        errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}`, msg: 'a role chain must be an array of "platform/model[@effort]" strings' });
    36	        continue;
    37	      }
    38	      /** @type {any[]} */
    39	      const entries = [];
    40	      for (let i = 0; i < chain.length; i += 1) {
    41	        const s = chain[i];
    42	        const slash = typeof s === 'string' ? s.indexOf('/') : -1;
    43	        if (slash <= 0 || slash === s.length - 1) {
    44	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `malformed chain entry ${JSON.stringify(s)} — expected "platform/model[@effort]"` });
    45	          continue;
    46	        }
    47	        const platform = s.slice(0, slash);
    48	        const rest = s.slice(slash + 1);
    49	        const at = rest.indexOf('@');
    50	        const model = at === -1 ? rest : rest.slice(0, at);
    51	        const effort = at === -1 ? null : rest.slice(at + 1);
    52	        if (parsed.platforms && !(platform in parsed.platforms)) {
    53	          errors.push({ path: `roles.${role}[${i}]`, msg: `unknown platform "${platform}" — not a key of config.platforms` });
    54	          continue;
    55	        }
    56	        entries.push({ platform, model, effort });
    57	      }
    58	      roles[role] = entries;
    59	    }
    60	  }
    61	
    62	  if (errors.length > 0) return { config: null, errors };
    63	  return { config: { ...parsed, roles }, errors };
    64	}

/bin/zsh -lc "nl -ba core/src/loop/children.mjs | sed -n '1,90p'" in /Users/saksham/baton
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	/**
     2	 * Child supervision — the loop's reliability surface. Owns the FULL child
     3	 * lifecycle: argv assembly (per-role permissions), detached process-group
     4	 * spawn with closed stdin, timeout SIGTERM→grace→SIGKILL of the whole group,
     5	 * capped transcript logs, region-bounded verdict parsing (prompt-echo-proof),
     6	 * and exit classification via core detect. Plan:
     7	 * docs/plans/2026-07-18-goal-loop-worktree-pipeline.md §"Child supervision
     8	 * contract".
     9	 */
    10	import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
    11	import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
    12	import { dirname } from 'node:path';
    13	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
    14	import { redactSecrets } from '../util/redact.mjs';
    15	
    16	// Every review seat is read-only — writers are the implementer-side roles
    17	// only. The merger CHILD is read-only too: it adversarially re-checks, but
    18	// the SUPERVISOR performs the actual merge via worktrees.mergeSubtask
    19	// (pipeline contract — the merger child never issues git).
    20	const REVIEWER_ROLES = new Set(['plan-reviewer', 'test-verifier', 'subtask-reviewer', 'final-reviewer-a', 'final-reviewer-b', 'merger']);
    21	
    22	// AGENTS.md §9: children commit solely as the repo owner, zero AI attribution.
    23	const GIT_IDENTITY = Object.freeze({
    24	  GIT_AUTHOR_NAME: 'SakshamUboweja',
    25	  GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com',
    26	  GIT_COMMITTER_NAME: 'SakshamUboweja',
    27	  GIT_COMMITTER_EMAIL: 'ssakshamu@gmail.com',
    28	});
    29	
    30	/**
    31	 * Assemble the spawn contract for a role child. Reviewers are read-only on
    32	 * every platform; every child is marked supervised (its own baton hooks
    33	 * no-op), carries the sole-author git identity, and has stdin CLOSED at
    34	 * spawn (the codex-exec stdin-hang field failure).
    35	 * @param {{platform: string, role: string, model: string, effort?: string | null, mode?: string}} assignment
    36	 * @param {string} prompt
    37	 * @param {{root: string, gitDir?: string}} opts
    38	 * @returns {{command: string, args: string[], env: Record<string, string>, stdio: any[], cwd: string}}
    39	 */
    40	export function buildChildArgv(assignment, prompt, opts) {
    41	  const readOnly = REVIEWER_ROLES.has(assignment.role);
    42	  const env = { BATON_SUPERVISED_CHILD: '1', ...GIT_IDENTITY };
    43	  const stdio = ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'];
    44	
    45	  if (assignment.platform === 'claude-code') {
    46	    // --model honors the role matrix (and entry-level avoidance);
    47	    // --output-format json is what parseVerdict's claude branch reads; the
    48	    // cwd puts the child in its seat, never the supervisor cwd.
    49	    const args = ['-p', prompt, '--model', assignment.model, '--output-format', 'json'];
    50	    if (readOnly) {
    51	      // Scoped read-only git so a reviewer can actually SEE the diff it
    52	      // reviews — still no write/edit capability.
    53	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'default', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Bash(git diff:*),Bash(git log:*),Bash(git show:*)');
    54	    } else {
    55	      args.push('--permission-mode', 'acceptEdits', '--allowedTools', 'Read,Grep,Glob,Write,Edit,Bash');
    56	    }
    57	    return { command: 'claude', args, env, stdio, cwd: opts.root };
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  // codex (and the default shape for exec-style CLIs).
    61	  const args = ['exec', '-C', opts.root, '-s', readOnly ? 'read-only' : 'workspace-write', '--model', assignment.model];
    62	  if (assignment.effort) args.push('-c', `model_reasoning_effort=${assignment.effort}`);
    63	  if (!readOnly && typeof opts.gitDir === 'string' && opts.gitDir.length > 0) {
    64	    // A linked worktree's index/lock live under the MAIN repo's .git —
    65	    // outside the seat-cwd sandbox. Without this grant a codex writer can
    66	    // edit but never commit (dogfood finding D2). Read-only children never
    67	    // get it.
    68	    args.push('-c', `sandbox_workspace_write.writable_roots=[${JSON.stringify(opts.gitDir)}]`);
    69	  }
    70	  args.push(prompt);
    71	  return { command: 'codex', args, env, stdio, cwd: opts.root };
    72	}
    73	
    74	const VERDICT_RE = /VERDICT:\s*(APPROVED_WITH_NOTES|APPROVED|BLOCKED)\b/g;
    75	
    76	/** @param {string} region @returns {{verdict: string, findings: string} | null} */
    77	function lastVerdictIn(region) {
    78	  let last = null;
    79	  for (const m of region.matchAll(VERDICT_RE)) last = m;
    80	  if (!last) return null;
    81	  const after = region.slice((last.index ?? 0) + last[0].length);
    82	  return { verdict: last[1], findings: after.trim() };
    83	}
    84	
    85	/**
    86	 * Region-bounded verdict parsing — the prompt region (which quotes the
    87	 * verdict format in its instructions) is NEVER scanned, and there is no
    88	 * whole-transcript fallback. Unparseable is BLOCKED, never APPROVED.
    89	 * - codex: only the text after the LAST line starting with 'tokens used'.
    90	 * - claude-code: only the `result` field of the structured JSON output.

    20	    if (probes && probes[platform]) {
    21	      const { capability, outcome } = probes[platform];
    22	      if (outcome === 'rate-limited') return 'rate-limited';
    23	      if (capability === 'not-installed') return 'not-installed';
    24	      // 'installed' means installation was PROVEN but the probe was capped there
    25	      // (capOnSuccess) — authentication is UNVERIFIED, not verified-failed. Only
    26	      // a probe that ran and did NOT succeed (outcome !== 'ok') is a verified
    27	      // auth/reachability failure worth skipping; a capped success stays
    28	      // selectable and is flagged degraded below (iter-3 F1, plan §Role matrix).
    29	      if (capability === 'installed' && outcome !== 'ok') return 'unauthenticated';
    30	    }
